document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  /*-----------CLICK PLAY BTN-----------*/
  document.querySelectorAll('.video-wrap').forEach(wrap => {
    const btn = wrap.querySelector('.video_play-btn');
    const poster = wrap.querySelector('.video_poster');
    const video = wrap.querySelector('video');

    if (!btn || !video) return;

    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      video.play();
      btn.style.display = 'none';
      if (poster) poster.style.display = 'none';
    });
  });
  /*-----------------------------------*/
  const DEBUG = true;
  const videoPage = window.location.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean).pop() || 'home';

  function track(data) {
    if (DEBUG) {
      console.log('[VIDEO TRACKING]', data);
    } else {
      window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
      window.dataLayer.push(data);
    }
  }

  document.querySelectorAll('video').forEach(video => {
    const milestones = { 25: false, 50: false, 75: false };
    const name = video.dataset.videoName || video.src;

    video.setAttribute('data-video-page', videoPage);

    video.addEventListener('play', () => {
      if (video.currentTime < 1) {
        track({
          event: 'video_start',
          video_title: name,
          video_duration: Math.round(video.duration),
          video_page: videoPage
        });
      }
    });

    video.addEventListener('timeupdate', () => {
      if (!video.duration) return;
      const pct = (video.currentTime / video.duration) * 100;

      [25, 50, 75].forEach(milestone => {
        if (!milestones[milestone] && pct >= milestone) {
          milestones[milestone] = true;
          track({
            event: 'video_progress',
            video_percent: milestone,
            video_title: name,
            video_duration: Math.round(video.duration),
            video_page: videoPage
          });
        }
      });
    });

    video.addEventListener('ended', () => {
      track({
        event: 'video_complete',
        video_title: name,
        video_duration: Math.round(video.duration),
        video_page: videoPage
      });
    });
  });
  /*------------------------------------------------*/
gsap.utils.toArray('.parallax').forEach((el) => {
  const speed = parseFloat(el.dataset.speed || '1');
  const direction = (el.dataset.direction || 'y').toLowerCase();
  const axis = direction === 'x' ? 'xPercent' : 'yPercent';
  const amplitude = 30 * speed;

  gsap.set(el, { scale: 1.2, transformOrigin: 'center center' });

  gsap.fromTo(el,
    { [axis]: -amplitude / 2 },
    {
      [axis]: amplitude / 2,
      ease: 'none',
      scrollTrigger: {
        trigger: el,
        start: 'top bottom',
        end: 'bottom top',
        scrub: 0.6,
        invalidateOnRefresh: true,
      },
    }
  );
});
});
</script></div></div><div data-wf--nav-top--variant="base" class="nav_fixed"><div data-animation="default" data-collapse="medium" data-duration="400" data-easing="ease" data-easing2="ease" role="banner" class="nav_component w-nav"><div class="nav_annouce"><div class="hide w-embed"><!-- ============================================================
     Nav Announce - couleur de fond dynamique via CMS
     La couleur de .nav_annoucement-wrap suit le champ CMS
     "Background color" (Option: Bleu / Violet / Vert), lu via
     l'attribut personnalise data-bg (a lier dans le Designer).
     ============================================================ -->
<style>
  /* Bleu */
[data-bg="Bleu"]   { background-color: #04286A; }
  /* Violet */
[data-bg="Violet"] { background-color: #7F0962; }
  /* Vert */
[data-bg="Vert"]   { background-color: #1A7F69; }
</style></div><div class="hide w-embed w-script"><!--
  NAV TOP - Marquee conditionnel + fermeture memorisee (session) + theme-color iOS + --nav-height dynamique
  Role : marquee si .annoucement_inner depasse 80% de l'ecran ; sinon centre statique.
         Fermeture (.annoucement_close) masque .nav_annouce pour la SESSION en cours :
           - session = visites espacees de moins de SESSION_TIMEOUT_MIN minutes (comme Google Analytics) ;
           - partagee entre onglets (localStorage + horodatage), prolongee a chaque page vue ;
           - nouvelle session (inactivite > timeout, retour le lendemain...) => la barre revient.
         Synchronise la barre d'etat iOS (theme-color) et recalcule --nav-height pour que le
         menu mobile (.navbar_links, top:var(--nav-height)) s'ouvre SOUS le header complet (annonce incluse).
  Cibles : .nav_fixed > .nav_component > (.nav_annouce [wrap>inner] + .nav_main[data-menu-wrap] > .navbar_links)
  Note : --nav-height est un custom property pose par la regle [data-menu-wrap]{--nav-height:4rem} ;
         on le surcharge en inline sur [data-menu-wrap] avec le bas reel de la barre nav.
  Deps : aucune (CSS + JS natif). Respecte prefers-reduced-motion. Pause au survol.
  Reglages : RATIO, SPEED, --nvmq-gap, REMEMBER_CLOSE, SESSION_TIMEOUT_MIN, BAR_CLOSED_COLOR, SYNC_NAV_HEIGHT.
-->
<style>
  /* Piste : shrink-to-fit hors marquee (reste centree par le wrap) */
  .nvmq_track{ display:inline-flex; align-items:center; column-gap:var(--nvmq-gap,4rem); }
  /* Groupe = une copie du contenu original (reprend le gap 1rem de .annoucement_inner) */
  .nvmq_grp{ display:flex; align-items:center; column-gap:1rem; flex:0 0 auto; }
  .nvmq_grp.is-clone{ display:none; }            /* 2e copie masquee hors marquee */

  /* Etat marquee actif (classe posee par le JS sur .annoucement_inner) */
  .annoucement_inner.is-nvmq{ display:block; width:100%; flex:1 1 auto; min-width:0; overflow:hidden;
    -webkit-mask-image:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,#000 3rem,#000 calc(100% - 3rem),transparent);
            mask-image:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,#000 3rem,#000 calc(100% - 3rem),transparent); }
  .annoucement_inner.is-nvmq .nvmq_track{ display:flex; width:max-content; will-change:transform;
    animation:nvmqScroll var(--nvmq-dur,20s) linear infinite; }
  .annoucement_inner.is-nvmq .nvmq_grp.is-clone{ display:flex; pointer-events:none; }
  .annoucement_inner.is-nvmq:hover .nvmq_track{ animation-play-state:paused; }

  @keyframes nvmqScroll{ from{ transform:translateX(0); } to{ transform:translateX(calc(var(--nvmq-shift,0px) * -1)); } }

  /* Accessibilite : pas de defilement si l'utilisateur refuse les animations */
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
    .annoucement_inner.is-nvmq .nvmq_track{ animation:none; }
  }
</style>
<script>
(function(){
  // --- Reglages ---
  var RATIO = 0.8;                        // active le marquee si une copie du contenu depasse 80% de la largeur ecran
  var SPEED = 60;                        // vitesse de defilement en px/seconde
  var REMEMBER_CLOSE = true;             // true = barre cachee pour la session apres fermeture ; false = desactive
  var SESSION_TIMEOUT_MIN = 30;          // duree d'inactivite (min) apres laquelle une nouvelle session commence
  var BAR_CLOSED_COLOR = '#ffffff';      // couleur de la barre d'etat iOS quand l'annonce est fermee
  var SYNC_NAV_HEIGHT = true;            // true = recalcule --nav-height (menu mobile) selon la hauteur reelle du header
  var MENU_WRAP_SELECTOR = '[data-menu-wrap]'; // element qui porte --nav-height (fallback .nav_main)

  // --- Memorisation "fermee pour la session" ---
  // On stocke l'horodatage de la derniere page vue barre fermee (localStorage => partage entre onglets).
  // Tant que l'ecart entre deux pages vues reste < SESSION_TIMEOUT_MIN, la session continue et l'horodatage est prolonge.
  var STORE_KEY = 'nvmq-annouce-closed-at';
  var TIMEOUT_MS = SESSION_TIMEOUT_MIN * 60 * 1000;

  // Ecrit "maintenant" (fermeture ou prolongation de session) ; protege contre le mode prive / stockage bloque
  function touchClosed(){ try{ localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, String(Date.now())); }catch(e){} }

  // Lit l'etat une seule fois par page : session encore valide => on prolonge ; expiree => on nettoie
  function readClosed(){
    if(!REMEMBER_CLOSE) return false;
    try{
      var ts = parseInt(localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY), 10);
      if(ts && (Date.now() - ts) < TIMEOUT_MS){ touchClosed(); return true; }
      localStorage.removeItem(STORE_KEY);
    }catch(e){}
    return false;
  }
  var CLOSED = readClosed();

  // Masque toutes les barres d'annonce presentes dans le DOM
  function hideBars(){ document.querySelectorAll('.nav_annouce').forEach(function(b){ b.style.display = 'none'; }); }

  // Barre d'etat iOS : cree/met a jour <meta name="theme-color">
  function setThemeColor(c){
    var m = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
    if(!m){ m = document.createElement('meta'); m.setAttribute('name','theme-color'); document.head.appendChild(m); }
    m.setAttribute('content', c);
  }

  // --nav-height : surcharge le custom property sur [data-menu-wrap] avec le bas reel de la barre nav
  // => le menu mobile (top:var(--nav-height)) s'ouvre sous le header complet, annonce visible ou non.
  function refreshNavHeight(){
    if(!SYNC_NAV_HEIGHT) return;
    var wrap = document.querySelector(MENU_WRAP_SELECTOR) || document.querySelector('.nav_main');
    if(wrap){ wrap.style.setProperty('--nav-height', Math.round(wrap.getBoundingClientRect().bottom) + 'px'); }
  }

  // Masquage immediat (synchrone) si deja ferme dans la session : evite le flash de la barre
  // (fonctionne quand l'embed est place dans/apres .nav_annouce ; sinon init() prend le relais)
  if(CLOSED){ hideBars(); setThemeColor(BAR_CLOSED_COLOR); }

  // Lit le gap inter-repetitions defini en CSS (--nvmq-gap sur .nvmq_track), fallback 64px
  function gapPx(track){ var v = parseFloat(getComputedStyle(track).columnGap); return isNaN(v) ? 64 : v; }

  // Observe la largeur d'une copie pour re-mesurer quand le contenu/la police change (sans boucle : le groupe ne depend pas de l'etat marquee)
  var ro = ('ResizeObserver' in window) ? new ResizeObserver(function(){ schedule(); }) : null;

  // Enveloppe une fois le contenu de .annoucement_inner dans une piste + 2 groupes identiques
  function build(inner){
    if(inner.__nvmq) return inner.__nvmq;
    var track = document.createElement('div'); track.className = 'nvmq_track';
    var grp = document.createElement('div'); grp.className = 'nvmq_grp';
    while(inner.firstChild){ grp.appendChild(inner.firstChild); }        // deplace le contenu original dans le groupe 1
    var clone = grp.cloneNode(true);                                     // groupe 2 = copie pour la boucle sans couture
    clone.classList.add('is-clone'); clone.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    track.appendChild(grp); track.appendChild(clone); inner.appendChild(track);
    inner.__nvmq = { track:track, grp:grp };
    if(ro) ro.observe(grp);
    return inner.__nvmq;
  }

  // Mesure et bascule statique <-> marquee selon le seuil
  function update(inner){
    var api = build(inner);
    var contentW = api.grp.getBoundingClientRect().width;               // largeur reelle d'une copie
    if(contentW > window.innerWidth * RATIO){                           // depasse le seuil -> marquee
      var shift = contentW + gapPx(api.track);                          // distance d'un raccord invisible
      inner.style.setProperty('--nvmq-shift', shift + 'px');
      inner.style.setProperty('--nvmq-dur', (shift / SPEED) + 's');
      inner.classList.add('is-nvmq');
    } else {                                                            // sous le seuil -> statique centre
      inner.classList.remove('is-nvmq');
      inner.style.removeProperty('--nvmq-shift');
      inner.style.removeProperty('--nvmq-dur');
    }
  }

  function updateAll(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.nav_annoucement-wrap .annoucement_inner').forEach(update);
    refreshNavHeight();                                                 // la hauteur du header peut avoir change
  }

  // Debounce commun (resize + ResizeObserver)
  var t; function schedule(){ clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(updateAll, 150); }

  // Init : au chargement, apres les fonts (la largeur depend de la police) et au resize
  function init(){
    if(CLOSED) hideBars();                                              // filet de securite si l'embed est hors de .nav_annouce
    updateAll();
    if(document.fonts && document.fonts.ready){ document.fonts.ready.then(updateAll); }
    window.addEventListener('resize', schedule);
    window.addEventListener('orientationchange', schedule);

    // Barre d'etat iOS : couleur de l'annonce si visible au chargement, sinon blanc
    var bar = document.querySelector('.nav_annouce');
    var wrap = document.querySelector('.nav_annoucement-wrap');
    if(bar && bar.style.display !== 'none' && wrap){ setThemeColor(getComputedStyle(wrap).backgroundColor); }
    else { setThemeColor(BAR_CLOSED_COLOR); }

    // Fermeture : masque la barre, demarre la session "fermee", remet la barre d'etat au blanc et recalcule --nav-height
    document.querySelectorAll('.annoucement_close').forEach(function(b){
      b.addEventListener('click', function(){
        var bar = b.closest('.nav_annouce'); if(bar) bar.style.display = 'none';
        setThemeColor(BAR_CLOSED_COLOR);
        if(REMEMBER_CLOSE) touchClosed();
        requestAnimationFrame(refreshNavHeight);                        // apres le reflow du display:none
      });
    });
  }
  if(document.readyState !== 'loading'){ init(); } else { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init); }
})();
</script></div><div class="collection-list-wrapper-9 w-dyn-list"><div role="list" class="w-dyn-items"><div data-bg="Bleu" role="listitem" class="nav_annoucement-wrap w-dyn-item"><div class="annoucement_inner"><div class="annoucement_txt">Sekoia Self-Hosted is now live.</div><div class="link"><div class="link-txt-inner">View more</div><div class="icn w-embed"><svg width="6" height="10" viewBox="0 0 6 10" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">

  }

    will-change: transform;

    inset: 0;

    inset: 0 0 auto;
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  .nav_backdrop {

    pointer-events: none;
  }

  /* Desktop : si un panel est plus haut que la viewport, il scrolle en interne */

<script>
  function initSekoiaNavDirectionalHover() {
    if (typeof gsap === 'undefined') {
      console.warn('[Sekoia Nav] GSAP not loaded');
      return;
    }

    // === Constants ===
    const DUR = {
      bgMorph: 0.4,
      contentIn: 0.3,
      contentOut: 0.2,
      stagger: 0.25,
      backdropIn: 0.3,
      backdropOut: 0.2,
      openScale: 0.35,
      closeScale: 0.25,
    };
    const HOVER_ENTER = 120;
    const HOVER_LEAVE = 150;
    const MOBILE_BP = 991;

    // === DOM ===
    const menuWrap = document.querySelector('[data-menu-wrap]');
    if (!menuWrap) return;

    const navList = document.querySelector('[data-nav-list]');
    const dropWrapper = document.querySelector('[data-dropdown-wrapper]');
    const dropContainer = document.querySelector('[data-dropdown-container]');
    const dropBg = document.querySelector('[data-dropdown-bg]');
    const backdrop = document.querySelector('[data-menu-backdrop]');
    const toggles = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-dropdown-toggle]')];
    const panels = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-nav-content]')];
    const burger = document.querySelector('[data-burger-toggle]');
    const burgerLines = document.querySelector('.navbar_burger-lines');
    const burgerClose = document.querySelector('.navbar_burger-close');
    const backBtn = document.querySelector('[data-mobile-back]');
    const logo = document.querySelector('[data-menu-logo]');

    // === State ===
    const state = {
      isOpen: false,
      activePanel: null,
      activePanelIndex: -1,
      isMobile: window.innerWidth <= MOBILE_BP,
      mobileMenuOpen: false,
      mobilePanelActive: null,
      hoverTimer: null,
      leaveTimer: null,
      tl: null,
      mobileTl: null,
      mobilePanelTl: null,
    };

    // === Helpers ===
    const getPanel = (name) => document.querySelector(`[data-nav-content="${name}"]`);
    const getToggle = (name) => document.querySelector(`[data-dropdown-toggle="${name}"]`);
    const getFade = (el) => (el ? el.querySelectorAll('[data-menu-fade]') : []);
    const getNavItems = () => (navList ? navList.querySelectorAll('[data-nav-list-item]') : []);
    const getIndex = (name) => toggles.indexOf(getToggle(name));
    const stagger = (n) => (n <= 1 ? 0 : { amount: DUR.stagger });

    function clearTimers() {
      clearTimeout(state.hoverTimer);
      clearTimeout(state.leaveTimer);
      state.hoverTimer = state.leaveTimer = null;
    }

    function killTl(key) {
      if (state[key]) {
        state[key].kill();
        state[key] = null;
      }
    }

    function killDropdown() {
      killTl('tl');
      if (dropContainer) gsap.killTweensOf(dropContainer);
      if (backdrop) gsap.killTweensOf(backdrop);
      panels.forEach((p) => {
        gsap.killTweensOf(p);
        gsap.killTweensOf(getFade(p));
      });
    }

    function killMobile() {
      killTl('mobileTl');
      gsap.killTweensOf([navList, burgerLines, burgerClose].filter(Boolean));
    }

    function killMobilePanel() {
      killTl('mobilePanelTl');
      gsap.killTweensOf(getNavItems());
      if (backBtn) gsap.killTweensOf(backBtn);
      if (logo) gsap.killTweensOf(logo);
      panels.forEach((p) => {
        gsap.killTweensOf(p);
        gsap.killTweensOf(getFade(p));
      });
    }

    function resetToggles() {
      toggles.forEach((t) => t.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'));
    }

    function resetDesktop() {
      panels.forEach((p) => {
        gsap.set(p, { visibility: 'hidden', opacity: 0, pointerEvents: 'none', xPercent: 0 });
        gsap.set(getFade(p), { autoAlpha: 0, x: 0, y: 0 });
      });
      if (dropContainer) gsap.set(dropContainer, { height: 0 });
      if (backdrop) gsap.set(backdrop, { autoAlpha: 0 });
      menuWrap.setAttribute('data-menu-open', 'false');
      resetToggles();
    }

    function setupMobile() {
      panels.forEach((p) => {
        gsap.set(p, { autoAlpha: 0, xPercent: 0, visibility: 'visible', pointerEvents: 'none' });
        gsap.set(getFade(p), { xPercent: 20, autoAlpha: 0 });
      });
      gsap.set(getNavItems(), { xPercent: 0, y: 0, autoAlpha: 1 });
      if (navList) gsap.set(navList, { autoAlpha: 0, x: 0 });
      if (backBtn) gsap.set(backBtn, { autoAlpha: 0 });
      if (logo) gsap.set(logo, { autoAlpha: 1 });
      if (dropContainer) gsap.set(dropContainer, { clearProps: 'height,all' });
      if (backdrop) gsap.set(backdrop, { autoAlpha: 0 });
    }

    function measurePanel(name) {
      const el = getPanel(name);
      if (!el) return 0;
      const s = el.style;
      const prev = [s.visibility, s.opacity, s.pointerEvents];
      Object.assign(s, { visibility: 'visible', opacity: '0', pointerEvents: 'none' });
      const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
      const h = el.getBoundingClientRect().height + (parseFloat(cs.marginTop) || 0) + (parseFloat(cs.marginBottom) || 0);
      [s.visibility, s.opacity, s.pointerEvents] = prev;
      return h;
    }

    // === DESKTOP — open dropdown ===
    function openDropdown(panelName) {
      if (state.isMobile) return;
      if (state.isOpen && state.activePanel === panelName) return;
      if (state.isOpen) return switchPanel(state.activePanel, panelName);

      const height = measurePanel(panelName);
      if (!height) return;

      killDropdown();
      resetDesktop();

      const el = getPanel(panelName);
      const fade = getFade(el);
      const toggle = getToggle(panelName);

      state.isOpen = true;
      state.activePanel = panelName;
      state.activePanelIndex = getIndex(panelName);
      menuWrap.setAttribute('data-menu-open', 'true');
      if (toggle) toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');

      if (dropWrapper) dropWrapper.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
      gsap.set(dropContainer, { height: 0 });

      const tl = gsap.timeline();
      state.tl = tl;
      if (backdrop) tl.to(backdrop, { autoAlpha: 1, duration: DUR.backdropIn, ease: 'power2.out' }, 0);
      tl.to(dropContainer, { height, duration: DUR.openScale, ease: 'power3.out' }, 0);
      tl.set(el, { visibility: 'visible', opacity: 1, pointerEvents: 'auto' }, 0.05);
      if (fade.length) {
        tl.fromTo(fade, { autoAlpha: 0, y: 8 }, { autoAlpha: 1, y: 0, duration: DUR.contentIn, stagger: stagger(fade.length), ease: 'power3.out' }, 0.1);
      }
    }

    // === DESKTOP — close dropdown ===
    function closeDropdown() {
      if (!state.isOpen) return;
      const el = getPanel(state.activePanel);
      const fade = el ? getFade(el) : [];

      killDropdown();

      const tl = gsap.timeline({
        onComplete() {
          state.isOpen = false;
          state.activePanel = null;
          state.activePanelIndex = -1;
          state.tl = null;
          if (dropWrapper) dropWrapper.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
          resetDesktop();
        },
      });
      state.tl = tl;
      if (fade.length) tl.to(fade, { autoAlpha: 0, y: -4, duration: DUR.contentOut * 0.7, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0);
      tl.to(dropContainer, { height: 0, duration: DUR.closeScale, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0.05);
      if (backdrop) tl.to(backdrop, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: DUR.backdropOut, ease: 'power2.out' }, 0);
      if (el) tl.set(el, { visibility: 'hidden', opacity: 0, pointerEvents: 'none' });
    }

    // === DESKTOP — switch panel (directional) ===
    function switchPanel(fromName, toName) {
      const dir = getIndex(toName) > getIndex(fromName) ? 1 : -1;
      const fromEl = getPanel(fromName);
      const toEl = getPanel(toName);
      if (!fromEl || !toEl) return;

      const fromFade = getFade(fromEl);
      const toFade = getFade(toEl);
      const toHeight = measurePanel(toName);
      if (!toHeight) return;

      killDropdown();

      panels.forEach((p) => {
        gsap.set(p, { visibility: 'hidden', opacity: 0, pointerEvents: 'none', xPercent: 0 });
        gsap.set(getFade(p), { autoAlpha: 0, x: 0, y: 0 });
      });
      gsap.set(fromEl, { visibility: 'visible', opacity: 1, pointerEvents: 'auto', x: 0 });
      if (fromFade.length) gsap.set(fromFade, { autoAlpha: 1, x: 0, y: 0 });
      if (backdrop) gsap.set(backdrop, { autoAlpha: 1 });

      const toToggle = getToggle(toName);
      state.activePanel = toName;
      state.activePanelIndex = getIndex(toName);
      resetToggles();
      if (toToggle) toToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');

      const xOut = dir * -30;
      const xIn = dir * 30;
      const tl = gsap.timeline();
      state.tl = tl;

      if (fromFade.length) tl.to(fromFade, { autoAlpha: 0, x: xOut, duration: DUR.contentOut, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0);
      tl.set(fromEl, { visibility: 'hidden', opacity: 0, pointerEvents: 'none', xPercent: 0 }, DUR.contentOut);
      if (fromFade.length) tl.set(fromFade, { x: 0 }, DUR.contentOut);
      tl.to(dropContainer, { height: toHeight, duration: DUR.bgMorph, ease: 'power3.out' }, 0.05);
      tl.set(toEl, { visibility: 'visible', opacity: 1, pointerEvents: 'auto', xPercent: 0 }, DUR.contentOut * 0.5);
      if (toFade.length) {
        tl.fromTo(toFade, { autoAlpha: 0, x: xIn }, { autoAlpha: 1, x: 0, duration: DUR.contentIn, stagger: stagger(toFade.length), ease: 'power3.out' }, DUR.contentOut * 0.6);
      }
    }

    // === HOVER INTENT ===
    function handleToggleEnter(e) {
      if (state.isMobile) return;
      const name = e.currentTarget.getAttribute('data-dropdown-toggle');
      if (!name) return;
      clearTimeout(state.leaveTimer);
      state.leaveTimer = null;
      clearTimeout(state.hoverTimer);
      state.hoverTimer = setTimeout(() => openDropdown(name), state.isOpen ? 0 : HOVER_ENTER);
    }

    function handleToggleLeave() {
      if (state.isMobile) return;
      clearTimeout(state.hoverTimer);
      state.hoverTimer = null;
      state.leaveTimer = setTimeout(closeDropdown, HOVER_LEAVE);
    }

    function handleWrapperEnter() {
      if (state.isMobile) return;
      clearTimeout(state.leaveTimer);
      state.leaveTimer = null;
    }

    function handleWrapperLeave() {
      if (state.isMobile) return;
      state.leaveTimer = setTimeout(closeDropdown, HOVER_LEAVE);
    }

    // === CLOSE BEHAVIORS ===
    function handleEscape(e) {
      if (e.key !== 'Escape') return;
      if (state.isMobile) {
        if (state.mobilePanelActive) closeMobilePanel();
        else if (state.mobileMenuOpen) closeMobileMenu();
        return;
      }
      if (state.isOpen) {
        const t = getToggle(state.activePanel);
        closeDropdown();
        if (t) t.focus();
      }
    }

    function handleDocClick(e) {
      if (state.isMobile || !state.isOpen) return;
      if (!e.target.closest('[data-menu-wrap]')) closeDropdown();
    }

    // === KEYBOARD NAV ===
    function focusFirstLink(panelName) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        const el = getPanel(panelName);
        if (!el) return;
        const link = el.querySelector('a');
        if (!link) return;
        gsap.set(link, { visibility: 'visible' });
        link.focus();
      }, 80);
    }

    function handleKeydownOnToggle(e) {
      if (state.isMobile) return;
      const name = e.currentTarget.getAttribute('data-dropdown-toggle');
      if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (state.isOpen && state.activePanel === name) closeDropdown();
        else {
          openDropdown(name);
          focusFirstLink(name);
        }
        return;
      }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!state.isOpen || state.activePanel !== name) openDropdown(name);
        focusFirstLink(name);
      }
    }

    // === MOBILE — burger swap (lines ↔ close, via CSS + fade GSAP) ===
    function animateBurger(toX) {
      const tl = gsap.timeline({ defaults: { ease: 'power2.inOut', duration: 0.2 } });
      if (toX) {
        if (burgerLines) tl.to(burgerLines, { autoAlpha: 0 }, 0);
        if (burgerClose) tl.fromTo(burgerClose, { autoAlpha: 0 }, { autoAlpha: 1 }, 0.1);
      } else {
        if (burgerClose) tl.to(burgerClose, { autoAlpha: 0 }, 0);
        if (burgerLines) tl.fromTo(burgerLines, { autoAlpha: 0 }, { autoAlpha: 1 }, 0.1);
      }
      return tl;
    }

    // === MOBILE — open/close menu ===
    function openMobileMenu() {
      killMobile();
      state.mobileMenuOpen = true;
      menuWrap.setAttribute('data-menu-open', 'true');
      if (burger) burger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');

      const items = getNavItems();
      const tl = gsap.timeline();
      state.mobileTl = tl;
      tl.add(animateBurger(true), 0);
      if (navList) tl.to(navList, { autoAlpha: 1, duration: 0.3, ease: 'power2.out' }, 0);
      if (items.length) {
        tl.fromTo(items, { autoAlpha: 0, y: 12 }, { autoAlpha: 1, y: 0, duration: 0.3, stagger: 0.04, ease: 'power3.out' }, 0.15);
      }
    }

    function closeMobileMenu() {
      const hadPanel = state.mobilePanelActive;
      const panelEl = hadPanel ? getPanel(hadPanel) : null;

      killMobile();
      killMobilePanel();
      menuWrap.setAttribute('data-menu-open', 'false');
      state.mobileMenuOpen = false;
      state.mobilePanelActive = null;
      if (burger) burger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');

      const tl = gsap.timeline({
        onComplete() {
          state.mobileTl = null;
          setupMobile();
        },
      });
      state.mobileTl = tl;
      tl.add(animateBurger(false), 0);
      if (hadPanel && panelEl) {
        tl.to(panelEl, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.3, ease: 'power2.inOut' }, 0.05);
        if (backBtn) tl.to(backBtn, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.2, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0.05);
      }
      if (navList) tl.to(navList, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.3, ease: 'power2.inOut' }, 0.05);
    }

    // === MOBILE — slide-over panels ===
    function openMobilePanel(panelName) {
      const el = getPanel(panelName);
      if (!el) return;
      killMobilePanel();
      state.mobilePanelActive = panelName;

      const navItems = getNavItems();
      const panelFade = getFade(el);
      const tl = gsap.timeline();
      state.mobilePanelTl = tl;

      if (navItems.length) {
        tl.to(navItems, { xPercent: -10, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.35, stagger: 0.03, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0);
      }
      if (logo) tl.to(logo, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.2, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0);
      if (backBtn) tl.to(backBtn, { autoAlpha: 1, duration: 0.25, ease: 'power2.inOut' }, 0.15);

      tl.set(el, { autoAlpha: 1, xPercent: 0, pointerEvents: 'auto' }, 0.2);
      if (panelFade.length) {
        tl.fromTo(panelFade, { xPercent: 8, autoAlpha: 0 }, { xPercent: 0, autoAlpha: 1, duration: 0.3, stagger: stagger(panelFade.length), ease: 'power3.out' }, 0.25);
      }
    }

    function closeMobilePanel() {
      if (!state.mobilePanelActive) return;
      const el = getPanel(state.mobilePanelActive);
      if (!el) return;
      killMobilePanel();

      const navItems = getNavItems();
      const panelFade = getFade(el);
      const tl = gsap.timeline({
        onComplete() {
          state.mobilePanelActive = null;
          state.mobilePanelTl = null;
        },
      });
      state.mobilePanelTl = tl;

      if (panelFade.length) {
        tl.to(el, { xPercent: 20, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.3, stagger: 0.02, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0);
      }
      tl.set(el, { autoAlpha: 0, pointerEvents: 'none' }, 0.25);
      if (backBtn) tl.to(backBtn, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.2, ease: 'power2.in' }, 0);
      if (logo) tl.to(logo, { autoAlpha: 1, duration: 0.25, ease: 'power2.out' }, 0.15);
      if (navItems.length) {
        tl.fromTo(navItems, { xPercent: -20, autoAlpha: 0 }, { xPercent: 0, autoAlpha: 1, duration: 0.35, stagger: 0.03, ease: 'power3.out' }, 0.25);
      }
    }

    function handleToggleClick(e) {
      if (!state.isMobile || !state.mobileMenuOpen) return;
      const name = e.currentTarget.getAttribute('data-dropdown-toggle');
      if (name) {
        e.preventDefault();
        openMobilePanel(name);
      }
    }

    // === RESIZE ===
    let resizeTimer = null;
    let lastWidth = window.innerWidth;
    function handleResize() {
      const w = window.innerWidth;
      if (w === lastWidth) return;
      lastWidth = w;
      clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
      resizeTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        const was = state.isMobile;
        state.isMobile = window.innerWidth <= MOBILE_BP;

        if (was && !state.isMobile) {
          killMobile();
          killMobilePanel();
          if (navList) gsap.set(navList, { clearProps: 'all' });
          gsap.set(getNavItems(), { clearProps: 'all' });
          if (backBtn) gsap.set(backBtn, { autoAlpha: 0 });
          if (logo) gsap.set(logo, { clearProps: 'all' });
          if (burgerLines) gsap.set(burgerLines, { clearProps: 'all' });
          if (burgerClose) gsap.set(burgerClose, { clearProps: 'all' });
          panels.forEach((p) => {
            gsap.set(p, { clearProps: 'all' });
            gsap.set(getFade(p), { clearProps: 'all' });
          });
          if (burger) burger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
          state.mobileMenuOpen = false;
          state.mobilePanelActive = null;
          resetDesktop();
        }
        if (!was && state.isMobile) {
          killDropdown();
          state.isOpen = false;
          state.activePanel = null;
          state.activePanelIndex = -1;
          clearTimers();
          menuWrap.setAttribute('data-menu-open', 'false');
          resetToggles();
          setupMobile();
        }
      }, 150);
    }

    // === EVENT BINDING ===
    toggles.forEach((btn) => {
      btn.addEventListener('mouseenter', handleToggleEnter);
      btn.addEventListener('mouseleave', handleToggleLeave);
      btn.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeydownOnToggle);
      btn.addEventListener('click', handleToggleClick);
    });
    if (dropWrapper) {
      dropWrapper.addEventListener('mouseenter', handleWrapperEnter);
      dropWrapper.addEventListener('mouseleave', handleWrapperLeave);
    }
    if (backdrop) backdrop.addEventListener('click', closeDropdown);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
    document.addEventListener('click', handleDocClick);
    if (burger) {
      burger.addEventListener('click', () => {
        state.mobileMenuOpen ? closeMobileMenu() : openMobileMenu();
      });
    }
    if (backBtn) backBtn.addEventListener('click', closeMobilePanel);
    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);

    // === INIT ===
    state.isMobile ? setupMobile() : resetDesktop();
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initSekoiaNavDirectionalHover);
</script></div><div class="w-embed"><style>
  @media (min-width: 992px) and (max-width: 1099px) {
  .nav_fixed .button-secondary {
    display: none;
  }
}
</style></div><div class="hide w-embed"><style>

  .nav_component.w-variant-566af11e-fba3-9f38-c1f9-d9baad4efb4d .panel_item .icn [fill="#212223"] {
    fill: currentColor;
  }

  /* Separateurs et barre CTA grise */
  .nav_component.w-variant-566af11e-fba3-9f38-c1f9-d9baad4efb4d .panel_divider {
    background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12);
  }
  .nav_component.w-variant-566af11e-fba3-9f38-c1f9-d9baad4efb4d .panel_cta_bar {
    background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);
  }

  /* Boutons des barres CTA : primaire inverse (blanc), secondaire outline clair */
  .nav_component.w-variant-566af11e-fba3-9f38-c1f9-d9baad4efb4d .panel_cta_bar .button {
    background-color: var(--greyscale--0);
    color: var(--greyscale--500);
  }
  .nav_component.w-variant-566af11e-fba3-9f38-c1f9-d9baad4efb4d .panel_cta_bar .button .btn_txt,
  .nav_component.w-variant-566af11e-fba3-9f38-c1f9-d9baad4efb4d .panel_cta_bar .button .btn_hover {
    color: var(--greyscale--500);
  }
  .nav_component.w-variant-566af11e-fba3-9f38-c1f9-d9baad4efb4d .panel_cta_bar .button-secondary {
    background-color: transparent;
    border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.24);
    color: var(--greyscale--0);
  }
  .nav_component.w-variant-566af11e-fba3-9f38-c1f9-d9baad4efb4d .panel_cta_bar .button-secondary .btn_txt,

    color: var(--greyscale--0);
  }
}

aria-hidden="true" data-search="fallbacks" class="srch_fallbacks"><img src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69aaa27bc8027f082ae84b86/6ab62024f7d63abe309a94fd_search-placeholder.svg" loading="lazy" data-search-fallback="platform" alt=""/><img src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69aaa27bc8027f082ae84b86/6ab62024f7d63abe309a94fd_search-placeholder.svg" loading="lazy" data-search-fallback="integrations" alt=""/><img src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69aaa27bc8027f082ae84b86/6ab62024f7d63abe309a94fd_search-placeholder.svg" loading="lazy" data-search-fallback="case-studies" alt=""/><img src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69aaa27bc8027f082ae84b86/6ab62024f7d63abe309a94fd_search-placeholder.svg" loading="lazy" data-search-fallback="solution-briefs" alt=""/><img src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69aaa27bc8027f082ae84b86/6ab62024f7d63abe309a94fd_search-placeholder.svg" loading="lazy" data-search-fallback="newsroom" alt=""/><p data-search-label="recent">Recent</p><p data-search-label="recommended">Recommended</p></div><div class="hide w-embed w-script"><!-- Sekoia smart search loader (about 1 KB) + state styles. PRODUCTION version, for the NAV TOP component.
     Loads the search UI only when a visitor is about to search: hover, focus or tap on a trigger.
     Triggers: [data-search-open] and the nav loupe (.nav_search).
     Depends on: the Search modal markup ([data-search="modal"]) and the Webflow Cloud app at SekoiaSearchAPI.
     Source of truth: webflow/search-embed.html in the Search-bar-Sekoia repo. -->
<style>
  /* Scroll lock while the modal is open (the modal script only toggles the class). The scrollbar's
     space stays reserved, so the page and the nav do not shift sideways when it disappears. */
  html.srch-lock, html.srch-lock body { overflow: hidden; }
  html.srch-lock { scrollbar-gutter: stable; }

  /* Nav loupe: dark grey on hover instead of full black (its SVG strokes with currentColor). */
  .nav_search { cursor: pointer; transition: color 200ms ease; }
  .nav_search:hover, .nav_search:focus-visible { color: var(--greyscale--400, #6f7376); }

  /* Nav loupe on tablet and mobile: below 992px the site hides it (".dropdown-toggle-2, .nav_search
     { display: none }"). A more specific !important shows it; it lands left of the language switch. */
  @media screen and (max-width: 991px) {
    .navbar_actions .nav_search { display: flex !important; }
  }

  /* Icon embeds: render SVGs as blocks so no text line box offsets them from their labels. */
  .srch_tab-icon svg, .srch_icon svg, .srch_close svg { display: block; }

  /* Filters (Figma states): inactive = icon, label and count text at 64%;
     hover and selected = 100%. Webflow classes cannot target a child from the parent state. */
  .srch_tab .srch_tab-label { opacity: 0.64; }
  .srch_tab .srch_count { color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--greyscale--500, #212223) 64%, transparent); }
  .srch_tab:hover .srch_tab-label, .srch_tab:focus-visible .srch_tab-label, .srch_tab.is-srch-active .srch_tab-label { opacity: 1; }
  .srch_tab:hover .srch_count, .srch_tab:focus-visible .srch_count, .srch_tab.is-srch-active .srch_count { color: var(--greyscale--500, #212223); }
  .srch_tab:focus-visible { outline: none; }

  /* Result row hover: grey background and the description turns dark. Set on the whole row so the
     card stays highlighted while the pointer is on its Recent remove cross (a sibling of the link). */
  .srch_item .is-srch-desc { transition: color 200ms ease; }
  .srch_row:hover .srch_item { background-color: var(--greyscale--100, #f0f3f6); }
  .srch_row:hover .is-srch-desc, .srch_item:focus-visible .is-srch-desc { color: var(--greyscale--500, #212223); }
</style>
<script>
(function () {
  var API = '/search-api';
  var TRIGGER = '[data-search-open], .nav_search';
  window.SekoiaSearchAPI = API;
  var loading = null;

  // The loupe is a plain div in the nav: make it reachable with Tab and announced by screen readers
  // (Webflow does not localize attributes, so the label follows the page language here).
  var label = /^fr/i.test(document.documentElement.lang) ? 'Rechercher' : 'Search';
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll(TRIGGER), function (el) {
    if (!el.hasAttribute('tabindex')) el.setAttribute('tabindex', '0');
    el.setAttribute('role', 'button');
    el.setAttribute('aria-label', label);
  });

  // Inject the UI script once; later calls reuse the same promise.
  function load() {
    if (!loading) {
      loading = new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
        var s = document.createElement('script');
        s.src = API + '/ui.js';
        s.async = true;
        s.onload = resolve;
        s.onerror = function () { loading = null; reject(); };
        document.head.appendChild(s);
      });
    }
    return loading;
  }

  // Preload on intent so the modal opens instantly on click.
  function intent(e) {
    if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest(TRIGGER)) load();
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerover', intent, { passive: true });
  document.addEventListener('focusin', intent);
  document.addEventListener('touchstart', intent, { passive: true });

  function openSearch() {
    load().then(function () { if (window.SekoiaSearch) window.SekoiaSearch.open(); });
  }
  // Open on click (the trigger may be a Webflow link, so block its default navigation).
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var trigger = e.target.closest && e.target.closest(TRIGGER);
    if (!trigger) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    openSearch();
  });
  // Keyboard: Enter or Space on the focused loupe opens the search too.
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key !== 'Enter' && e.key !== ' ') return;
    var trigger = e.target.closest && e.target.closest(TRIGGER);
    if (!trigger || trigger.tagName === 'A' || trigger.tagName === 'BUTTON') return; // native click already fires
    e.preventDefault();
    openSearch();
  });
})();
</script>
</div></div></div><main class="main-wrapper"><section class="section_hero"><div class="degrad-wrap"><div class="degrad-etoile is-1"></div><div class="degrad-etoile is-2"></div><div class="degrad-etoile is-4"></div><div class="w-embed"><style>

    position: absolute;
    left: min(25rem, 25%);
    top: 100%;

    height: 700vh;
    margin-top: -1500px;
    transform-origin: 0 1500px;   /* x = arete gauche, y = 280px : le pivot reste sur le point */
    mix-blend-mode: darken;
  }
  .degrad-etoile.is-1 { transform: rotate(-180deg); }
  .degrad-etoile.is-2 { transform: rotate(150deg); }
  .degrad-etoile.is-3 { transform: rotate(120deg); }
  /* Barre 4 : bande horizontale pleine largeur, sans overshoot ni rotation */
  .degrad-etoile.is-4 {
   background: linear-gradient(180deg, #45BFFF 0%, rgba(211, 232, 255, 0) 70%);
    left: 0;
    top: 100%;
    width: 100%;
    height: 8.3rem;
    margin-top: 0;
    transform: none;
  }

    left: 10%;
    top: 100%;

    top: 100%;

    transform-origin: 0 1000px;
}
}

</style></div></div><div class="padding-global padding-section-hero"><div class="container-large"><div class="hero_grid"><div class="hero_heading-wrap"><div class="hero_gartner"></div><h1 class="heading-style-h1 is-hero">Think beyond the threat</h1><p>Reduce risk. Slash response times. Cut operational costs. Sekoia’s autonomous AI agents detect, investigate, and contain threats before they disrupt your business.</p><div class="hero_cta-wrap"><a data-wf--btn-primary--variant="base" href="/contact" class="button w-inline-block"><p class="btn_txt">Get a demo</p><div class="btn_hover"></div></a></div></div></div></div></div><div class="hide w-embed w-script"><script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  const q = (s) => document.querySelector(s);

  const exitXDesktop = () => innerWidth;

  const exitXMobile = () => {
    const els = [q('.degrad-etoile.is-1'), q('.degrad-etoile.is-2')].filter(Boolean);
    if (!els.length) return innerWidth;
    return Math.max(...els.map(el => innerWidth - el.getBoundingClientRect().left + el.offsetWidth));
  };

  const stConfig = {
    trigger: '.section_hero',
    start: 'top top',
    endTrigger: '.section_hero-follow',
    end: 'top top',
    scrub: 0.6,
    invalidateOnRefresh: true,
  };

  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();

  // ─── DESKTOP : > 767px ────────────────────────────────────────────
  // top: 100% — left: min(25rem, 25%) — transform-origin: 0 1500px
  mm.add('(min-width: 768px)', () => {
    const tl = gsap.timeline({ scrollTrigger: stConfig });

    if (q('.degrad-wrap'))
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-wrap', { yPercent: 0 }, { yPercent: 100 }, 0);

    if (q('.degrad-etoile.is-1'))
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-1',
        { top: '100%', x: 0, rotation: -180, rotationY: 0 },
        { top: '6rem', x: exitXDesktop, rotation: -180, rotationY: 0 }, 0);

    if (q('.degrad-etoile.is-2')) {
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-2',
        { top: '100%', x: 0, rotationY: 0 },
        { top: '6rem', x: exitXDesktop, rotationY: 0 }, 0);
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-2',
        { rotation: 150 },
        { rotation: 180, ease: 'power2.out', duration: 0.05 }, 0);
    }

    if (q('.degrad-etoile.is-4'))
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-4',
        { top: '100%', y: '0%' },
        { top: '6rem', y: '-100%' }, 0);

    if (q('.hero_grid'))
      tl.fromTo('.hero_grid', { opacity: 1 }, { opacity: 0, duration: 0.2 }, 0);
  });

  // ─── MOBILE LANDSCAPE : 480px - 767px ─────────────────────────────
  // top: 100% — left: 10%
  mm.add('(max-width: 767px) and (min-width: 480px)', () => {
    const tl = gsap.timeline({ scrollTrigger: stConfig });

    if (q('.degrad-wrap'))
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-wrap', { yPercent: 0 }, { yPercent: 100 }, 0);

    if (q('.degrad-etoile.is-1'))
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-1',
        { top: '100%', x: 0, rotation: -180, rotationY: 0 },
        { top: '6rem', x: exitXMobile, rotation: -180, rotationY: 0 }, 0);

    if (q('.degrad-etoile.is-2')) {
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-2',
        { top: '100%', x: 0, rotationY: 0 },
        { top: '6rem', x: exitXMobile, rotationY: 0 }, 0);
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-2',
        { rotation: 150 },
        { rotation: 180, ease: 'power2.out', duration: 0.3 }, 0);
    }

    if (q('.degrad-etoile.is-4'))
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-4',
        { top: '100%', y: '0%' },
        { top: '6rem', y: '-100%' }, 0);

    if (q('.hero_grid'))
      tl.fromTo('.hero_grid', { opacity: 1 }, { opacity: 0, duration: 0.2 }, 0);
  });

  // ─── MOBILE PORTRAIT : < 479px ────────────────────────────────────
  // top: 100% — left: 0% — transform-origin: 0 1000px — margin-top: -1000px
  mm.add('(max-width: 479px)', () => {
    const tl = gsap.timeline({ scrollTrigger: stConfig });

    if (q('.degrad-wrap'))
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-wrap', { yPercent: 0 }, { yPercent: 100 }, 0);

    if (q('.degrad-etoile.is-1'))
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-1',
        { top: '100%', x: 0, rotation: -180, rotationY: 0 },
        { top: '6rem', x: exitXMobile, rotation: -180, rotationY: 0 }, 0);

    if (q('.degrad-etoile.is-2')) {
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-2',
        { top: '100%', x: 0, rotationY: 0 },
        { top: '6rem', x: exitXMobile, rotationY: 0 }, 0);
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-2',
        { rotation: 150 },
        { rotation: 180, ease: 'power2.out', duration: 0.05 }, 0);
    }

    if (q('.degrad-etoile.is-4'))
      tl.fromTo('.degrad-etoile.is-4',
        { top: '100%', y: '0%' },
        { top: '6rem', y: '-100%' }, 0);

    if (q('.hero_grid'))
      tl.fromTo('.hero_grid', { opacity: 1 }, { opacity: 0, duration: 0.2 }, 0);
  });

});

  id="motion-hero"
  src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69aaa27bc8027f082ae84b86/6a85737ab41c60f050422dca_Sekoia_AnimationHero.json"

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  const player = document.querySelector('#motion-hero');
  console.log('[motion-hero] init, lottie found:', !!player);
  if (!player) {
    console.warn('[motion-hero] #motion-hero introuvable dans le DOM');
    return;
  }

  // Le player Lottie doit être prêt avant qu'on le pilote ou qu'on le mesure
  function onReady(cb) {
    if (player.getLottie && player.getLottie()) { cb(); return; }
    player.addEventListener('ready', cb, { once: true });
  }

  onReady(function () {
    console.log('[motion-hero] lottie prêt');

    const anim = player.getLottie();        // instance lottie-web sous-jacente
    const fr = anim.frameRate || 30;        // images/seconde de l'anim
    const startFrame = fr * 1;              // 1 seconde

    // Démarre figé à 1 s
    player.pause();
    player.seek(startFrame);

    // ─── PLAY AU SCROLL ───────────────────────────────────────────
    ScrollTrigger.create({
      trigger: '.section_hero-follow',
      start: 'top 30%',
      onEnter: () => {
        console.log('[motion-hero] onEnter -> play() depuis 1s');
        player.seek(startFrame);  // garantit le point de départ
        player.play();
      },
      onEnterBack: () => {
        console.log('[motion-hero] onEnterBack -> play()');
        player.play();
      },
    });
    console.log('[motion-hero] ScrollTrigger créé');

    // ─── CENTRAGE STICKY ──────────────────────────────────────────
    // Centre .motion-hero-wrap verticalement dans le viewport (position sticky)
    function centerStickyVideo() {
      const wrap = document.querySelector('.motion-hero-wrap');
      if (!wrap) return;
      wrap.style.top = (window.innerHeight - wrap.offsetHeight) / 2 + 'px';
    }
    centerStickyVideo();
    window.addEventListener('resize', centerStickyVideo);

    // ─── ANIMATION D'EXPANSION (scale) ────────────────────────────
    // Scale jusqu'à la largeur de .hero_video-slot sans la dépasser
    const wrap = document.querySelector('.motion-hero-wrap');
    const slot = document.querySelector('.hero_video-slot');
    if (wrap && slot) {
      const scaleTarget = slot.offsetWidth / wrap.offsetWidth;
      gsap.set(wrap, { transformOrigin: 'center center' });
      gsap.to(wrap, {
        scale: scaleTarget,
        ease: 'none',
        scrollTrigger: {
          trigger: '.hero_motion-placeholder',
          start: 'top top',
          end: 'top -30%',
          scrub: 0.6,
        },
      });
      // les mesures peuvent changer une fois le Lottie rendu
      ScrollTrigger.refresh();
    }
  });
});
</script></div></div></div></div></div></section><section class="section"><div class="padding-global padding-section-large"><div class="container-large"><div class="companies_content"><div sizes="100vw" srcset="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69aaa27bc8027f082ae84b86/6a1c0e45cb2062e811e6f7f4_respond-2-3-p-500.webp 500w, https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69aaa27bc8027f082ae84b86/6a1c0e45cb2062e811e6f7f4_respond-2-3.webp 1092w" class="modular_img"/></div></div></div><img is-blue"/></div></div></div><div class="triggers"><div class="trigger"><div class="trigger-inner"><div>TRIGGER_INNER</div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="w-embed"><style>
  html.wf-design-mode .modular_right {
    max-height: none;
  }
  html.wf-design-mode .modular_img-slot-wrap {
    position: static;
    }
  html.wf-design-mode .modular_body.is-active {
    align-items: start;
    }
</style></div><div class="hide w-embed w-script"><script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 1. RÉCUPÉRATION DES ÉLÉMENTS DE BASE
  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const section = document.querySelector('.section_modular');
  if (!section) return;

  const trigger = section.querySelector('.trigger');
  const bodies = section.querySelectorAll('.modular_body');
  const sticky = section.querySelector('.modular-sticky');
  const tabs = section.querySelector('.modular_tabs');

  // Garde-fou : aligne le nombre de .modular-tab-wrap sur le nombre de .modular_body
  const existingTabWraps = section.querySelectorAll('.modular-tab-wrap');
  if (existingTabWraps.length > bodies.length) {
    for (let k = existingTabWraps.length - 1; k >= bodies.length; k--) {
      existingTabWraps[k].remove();
    }
  }
  const tabWraps = section.querySelectorAll('.modular-tab-wrap');

  const modulars = section.querySelector('.modulars');
  const triggersWrap = section.querySelector('.triggers');
  if (!trigger || !bodies.length) return;

  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 2. DUPLICATION DES .trigger POUR MATCHER LE NOMBRE DE .modular_body
  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  for (let i = 1; i < bodies.length; i++) {
    trigger.parentNode.appendChild(trigger.cloneNode(true));
  }

  const triggers = section.querySelectorAll('.trigger');
  const rights = section.querySelectorAll('.modular_right');

  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 3. HELPERS
  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Détection mobile au chargement (le mode ne change pas dynamiquement au resize)
  const isMobile = () => window.innerWidth < 768;

  const applyMediaHeights = (body) => {
    const media = body.querySelector('.modular_media');
    const imgWraps = body.querySelectorAll('.modular_img-wrap');
    if (!media || !imgWraps.length) return;
    const h = media.offsetHeight;
    imgWraps.forEach((wrap) => {
      wrap.style.height = `${h}px`;
    });
  };

  const setModularsMinHeight = () => {
    if (!modulars) return;
    if (isMobile()) {
      modulars.style.minHeight = '';
      modulars.style.height = '';
      return;
    }
    const stickyHeight = sticky ? sticky.offsetHeight : 0;
    const h = triggersWrap.offsetHeight + stickyHeight;
    modulars.style.minHeight = `${h}px`;
    modulars.style.height = `${h}px`;
  };

  const centerSticky = () => {
    if (!sticky) return;
    // Sur mobile, pas de sticky ni de tabs positionnés en absolu
    if (isMobile()) return;
    const reference = bodies[0];
    const refHeight = reference ? reference.offsetHeight : sticky.offsetHeight;
    const top = (window.innerHeight - refHeight) / 2;
    sticky.style.top = `${top}px`;
    if (tabs) {
      tabs.style.top = `${top}px`;
    }
  };

  const setSectionPadding = () => {
    // Plus de padding-bottom de "libération" en bas de section : la hauteur de
    // .modulars inclut déjà une hauteur de sticky pour le décollage. L'ancien
    // padding (= hauteur du dernier body) faisait double emploi et créait un gros
    // vide blanc en bas. On retire toute surcharge inline (on garde le CSS d'origine).
    section.style.paddingBottom = '';
  };

  const setActiveBody = (index) => {
    bodies.forEach((body, k) => {
      body.classList.toggle('is-active', k === index);
    });
    tabWraps.forEach((wrap, k) => {
      wrap.classList.toggle('is-active', k === index);
    });
  };

  // On stocke un "contrôleur" par body pour pouvoir le réinitialiser
  // quand le sticky passe d'un body à l'autre (voir bloc 5).
  const controllers = [];

  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 4. BOUCLE PRINCIPALE (un passage par body)
  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  triggers.forEach((trig, i) => {
    const body = bodies[i];
    if (!body) return;

    const features = body.querySelectorAll('.modular_feature');
    const inner = trig.querySelector('.trigger-inner');
    if (!inner || !features.length) return;

    // 4.1 Duplique .trigger-inner pour matcher le nombre de .modular_feature.
    //     ⚠️ Les .trigger-inner ne pilotent plus rien : ils servent uniquement à
    //     donner sa hauteur au .trigger, donc la LONGUEUR DE SCROLL allouée à ce body.
    for (let j = 1; j < features.length; j++) {
      inner.parentNode.appendChild(inner.cloneNode(true));
    }

    const expands = Array.from(features).map((f) => f.querySelector('.modular_feature-expand'));
    const imgWraps = body.querySelectorAll('.modular_img-wrap');
    const right = rights[i];

    // 4.2 État initial de l'accordéon (1ʳᵉ feature ouverte, les autres fermées)
    expands.forEach((exp, k) => {
      if (!exp) return;
      gsap.set(exp, {
        height: k === 0 ? 'auto' : 0,
        overflow: 'hidden',
      });
    });
    features.forEach((feature, k) => {
      gsap.set(feature, { opacity: k === 0 ? 1 : 0.2 });
    });

    // 4.3 setActive : ouvre l'accordéon `index`, dim les autres, sync les img-wrap.
    const setActive = (index) => {
      expands.forEach((exp, k) => {
        if (!exp) return;
        gsap.to(exp, {
          height: k === index ? 'auto' : 0,
          duration: 0.5,
          ease: 'power2.out',
          overwrite: true,
        });
      });
      features.forEach((feature, k) => {
        gsap.to(feature, {
          opacity: index === -1 ? 1 : k === index ? 1 : 0.2,
          duration: 0.5,
          ease: 'power2.out',
          overwrite: true,
        });
      });
      // is-active sur les .modular_img-wrap (sur mobile = swap d'image)
      imgWraps.forEach((wrap, k) => {
        wrap.classList.toggle('is-active', k === index);
      });
    };

    // 4.4 Hauteurs média + état initial des wraps (seul le premier = is-active)
    applyMediaHeights(body);
    imgWraps.forEach((wrap, k) => {
      wrap.classList.toggle('is-active', k === 0);
    });

    // 4.5 PARALLAX AU CLICK (plus aucun scrub au scroll).
    //     On déplace .modular_right d'un "écran média" par feature : la feature j
    //     révèle la portion j de l'image (plafonné quand on atteint le bas).
    if (right && !isMobile()) gsap.set(right, { yPercent: 0 });

    const moveParallax = (j) => {
      if (isMobile() || !right) return;
      const media = body.querySelector('.modular_media');
      const mediaH = media ? media.offsetHeight : 0;
      const rightH = right.offsetHeight;
      const totalShift = rightH - mediaH;
      let yPercent = 0;
      if (totalShift > 0 && mediaH > 0) {
        const p = Math.min(1, (j * mediaH) / totalShift);
        yPercent = -p * (totalShift / rightH) * 100;
      }
      gsap.to(right, {
        yPercent,
        duration: 0.6,
        ease: 'power2.out',
        overwrite: true,
      });
    };

    // 4.6 Barre de progression : animée AU SCROLL (scrub) sur la longueur du body,
    //     du point d'activation de ce body jusqu'à celui du body suivant.
    const tabWrap = tabWraps[i];
    const timelineActive = tabWrap ? tabWrap.querySelector('.modular_tab-timeline-active') : null;
    if (timelineActive && !isMobile()) {
      const nextTrig = triggers[i + 1];
      gsap.set(timelineActive, { width: '0%' });
      gsap.to(timelineActive, {
        width: '100%',
        ease: 'none',
        scrollTrigger: {
          trigger: trig,
          start: 'top bottom',
          endTrigger: nextTrig || trig,
          end: nextTrig ? 'top bottom' : 'bottom bottom',
          scrub: 0.6,
          invalidateOnRefresh: true,
        },
      });
    }

    // 4.7 Réinitialisation complète du body (appelée quand le sticky (ré)active ce body)
    const reset = (j = 0) => {
      setActive(j);
      moveParallax(j);
    };

    // 4.8 CLICK sur une feature → accordéon + parallax + timeline (desktop ET mobile,
    //     moveParallax/setTimeline ne font rien sur mobile).
    features.forEach((feature, j) => {
      feature.style.cursor = 'pointer';
      feature.addEventListener('click', () => {
        setActive(j);
        moveParallax(j);
      });
    });

    // ⚠️ On expose setActive et moveParallax séparément de reset :
    //    lors d'un changement de body, on veut réinitialiser l'accordéon (setActive)
    //    SANS rappeler moveParallax (overwrite:true) qui tuerait le slide d'entrée du right.
    controllers[i] = { reset, setActive, moveParallax };
  });

  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 5. SCROLL = PASSAGE DE BODY EN BODY (DESKTOP UNIQUEMENT)
  //    Au changement, on réinitialise l'accordéon du body sur sa 1ʳᵉ feature.
  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Transition entre bodies : le BODY ne slide plus (crossfade d'opacité).
  // Seul le .modular_right glisse horizontalement façon slider.
  let currentBody = -1;
  const SLIDE_DUR = 0.7;
  const SLIDE_EASE = 'power3.out';
  const FIRST_BODY_EXTRA_VH = 75; // course de scroll en plus pour le 1er body (en % de viewport)

  // Rallonge le 1er .trigger : le body 0 perd de la course pendant l'engagement
  // du sticky, on lui en redonne pour qu'il ne passe pas trop vite au body 1.
  // Repousse d'autant l'activation des bodies suivants (espacement préservé).
  const padFirstTrigger = () => {
    if (!triggers[0]) return;
    triggers[0].style.paddingBottom = `${(window.innerHeight * FIRST_BODY_EXTRA_VH) / 100}px`;
  };

  // Empile les bodies au même endroit + masque le débordement, pour qu'ils
  // puissent se superposer en crossfade. Hauteur du conteneur = body le plus grand
  // (sinon il s'effondrerait, tous les bodies étant en position absolute).
  const stackBodies = () => {
    const wrap = bodies[0].parentNode;
    // ⚠️ Ne PAS écraser un position:sticky (ou relative/absolute) existant :
    //    on ne force `relative` que si le conteneur est en `static`. Un élément
    //    sticky sert déjà de référent pour ses enfants en absolute.
    if (getComputedStyle(wrap).position === 'static') {
      wrap.style.position = 'relative';
    }
    wrap.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    let maxH = 0;
    bodies.forEach((b) => { maxH = Math.max(maxH, b.offsetHeight); });
    wrap.style.height = `${maxH}px`;
    bodies.forEach((b) => {
      b.style.position = 'absolute';
      b.style.top = '0';
      b.style.left = '0';
      b.style.width = '100%';
      // Masque le débordement du .modular_right pendant son slide d'entrée/sortie.
      b.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    });
  };

  // dir = 1  → le right sortant part vers la GAUCHE, le right entrant arrive par la DROITE
  // dir = -1 → miroir (utilisé au scroll vers le haut)
  const goToBody = (index, dir) => {
    if (index === currentBody || index < 0 || index >= bodies.length) return;
    const direction = dir || (index > currentBody ? 1 : -1);
    const incoming = bodies[index];
    const outgoing = bodies[currentBody];
    const incomingRight = rights[index];
    const outgoingRight = rights[currentBody];

    bodies.forEach((b, k) => b.classList.toggle('is-active', k === index));
    tabWraps.forEach((w, k) => w.classList.toggle('is-active', k === index));

    // Le BODY ne slide plus : simple crossfade, aucune translation horizontale.
    gsap.set(incoming, { xPercent: 0, x: 0 });
    gsap.to(incoming, { autoAlpha: 1, duration: SLIDE_DUR, ease: SLIDE_EASE });
    if (outgoing && outgoing !== incoming) {
      gsap.to(outgoing, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: SLIDE_DUR, ease: SLIDE_EASE });
    }

    // SLIDE uniquement sur le .modular_right.
    // yPercent:0 dans le "from" garantit que le parallax repart de zéro,
    // et overwrite:true tue l'ancien tween de parallax du right.
    if (incomingRight) {
      gsap.fromTo(
        incomingRight,
        { xPercent: 100 * direction, yPercent: 0 },
        { xPercent: 0, duration: SLIDE_DUR, ease: SLIDE_EASE, overwrite: true }
      );
    }
    if (outgoingRight && outgoingRight !== incomingRight) {
      gsap.to(outgoingRight, {
        xPercent: -100 * direction,
        duration: SLIDE_DUR,
        ease: SLIDE_EASE,
        overwrite: true,
        onComplete: () => gsap.set(outgoingRight, { xPercent: 0, yPercent: 0 }),
      });
    }

    currentBody = index;
    // Réinitialise l'accordéon du nouveau body SANS toucher au transform du right.
    if (controllers[index]) controllers[index].setActive(0);
  };

  if (!isMobile()) {
    stackBodies();
    padFirstTrigger();
    // État initial : tous les bodies à leur place (xPercent 0), seul le body 0 visible.
    bodies.forEach((b, k) => {
      gsap.set(b, { xPercent: 0, x: 0, autoAlpha: k === 0 ? 1 : 0 });
    });
    // Tous les rights à zéro (le right du body 0 est en place, prêt).
    rights.forEach((r) => { if (r) gsap.set(r, { xPercent: 0 }); });

    currentBody = 0;
    bodies[0].classList.add('is-active');
    if (tabWraps[0]) tabWraps[0].classList.add('is-active');
    if (controllers[0]) controllers[0].reset(0);

    triggers.forEach((trig, i) => {
      const activationST = ScrollTrigger.create({
        trigger: trig,
        start: 'top bottom',
        onEnter: () => goToBody(i, 1),
        onLeaveBack: () => goToBody(i - 1, -1),
      });

      // Click sur l'onglet → scroll jusqu'au point d'activation du body i
      const tabWrap = tabWraps[i];
      if (tabWrap) {
        tabWrap.style.cursor = 'pointer';
        tabWrap.addEventListener('click', () => {
          let target;
          if (i === 0) {
            // Body 0 est actif dès l'entrée dans la section : son `start` ('top bottom')
            // tombe AVANT le collage du sticky → on viserait trop haut.
            // On cible plutôt le point où le sticky s'engage (= body 0 centré).
            const stickyTop = sticky ? parseFloat(getComputedStyle(sticky).top) || 0 : 0;
            const ref = modulars || section;
            const refY = ref.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY;
            target = Math.max(0, refY - stickyTop);
          } else {
            target = activationST.start + 1;
          }
          window.scrollTo({ top: target, behavior: 'smooth' });
        });
      }
    });

    // Fade-out des onglets quand le dernier body finit de défiler (fin du pin).
    // On scrube l'opacité de .modular_tabs sur le dernier écran de scroll.
    if (tabs && triggers.length) {
      const lastTrig = triggers[triggers.length - 1];
      gsap.to(tabs, {
        autoAlpha: 0, // opacité 0 + masque les clics une fois invisible
        ease: 'none',
        scrollTrigger: {
          trigger: lastTrig,
          start: 'bottom bottom', // commence quand le bas du dernier trigger atteint le bas du viewport
          end: 'bottom top', // terminé (opacité 0) quand ce même bord atteint le haut
          scrub: 0.6,
          invalidateOnRefresh: true,
        },
      });
    }
  }

  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 6. INITIALISATION
  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  setModularsMinHeight();
  setSectionPadding();
  centerSticky();

  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // 7. RESIZE
  // ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    bodies.forEach((body) => applyMediaHeights(body));
    if (!isMobile()) {
      stackBodies();
      padFirstTrigger();
    }
    setModularsMinHeight();
    setSectionPadding();
    centerSticky();
    ScrollTrigger.refresh();
  });
});
</script></div></section><section class="section_review-quote"><div class="padding-global"><div class="container-large"><div class="review-2_content"><div class="review-heading-wrap"><h2 class="heading-style-h2">Sekoia for your MSSP team</h2><a data-wf--btn-secondary--variant="base" href="/solutions/mssp" class="button-secondary w-inline-block"><p class="btn_txt">See MSSP USE CASE</p><div class="btn_hover"></div></a></div><div class="review-quote_card-2"><div class="review-quote_inner-2"><div class="review-quote_left"><div class="div-block-15"><h3 class="review-quote_text is-max-width">“We chose Sekoia as we were re-architecting our platform for managed XDR. class="pills_favicon"/><div class="subtitle">Veeam Backup</div></div><div class="pill"><img loading="lazy" src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69aaa27bc8027f082ae84b86/6a267c949c1d67e7d82c0385_Anomali%20ThreatStream%20favicon.webp" alt="Anomali ThreatStream logo" class="pills_favicon"/><div class="subtitle">Anomali ThreatStream</div></div></div></div></div><div class="hide w-embed w-script"><script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const container = document.querySelector('.pills-container');
  if (!container) return;

  // Les pills existent déjà dans le DOM (instances du composant Pill, peuplées).
  // On NE clone plus : on travaille avec le nombre RÉEL de .pill présentes.
  const allPills = gsap.utils.toArray('.pill');
  if (!allPills.length) return;

  const isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768;

  // Limite optionnelle sur mobile : on masque le surplus et on l'exclut de l'anim.
  // Passer à Infinity (ou une valeur >= au nombre de pills) pour toutes les afficher.
  const MOBILE_MAX = 12;
  let pills = allPills;
  if (isMobile && allPills.length > MOBILE_MAX) {
    allPills.slice(MOBILE_MAX).forEach((p) => gsap.set(p, { display: 'none' }));
    pills = allPills.slice(0, MOBILE_MAX);
  }

  // Nombre de pills réellement animées (pilote la grille et les positions).
  const total = pills.length;

  // 2. Calcul des positions
  function buildPositions() {
    const isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768;
    const cols = isMobile ? 3 : 7;
    const rows = Math.ceil(total / cols);
    const halfW = window.innerWidth * (isMobile ? 0.45 : 0.6);
    const halfH = window.innerHeight * (isMobile ? 0.45 : 0.55);
    const minDistance = isMobile ? 130 : 160;
    const wrap = document.querySelector('.pills_heading-wrap');
    const rect = wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
    const excludeW = (rect.width / 2) + 20;
    const excludeH = (rect.height / 2) + 20;
    const placed = [];
    return pills.map((_, i) => {
      const col = i % cols;
      const row = Math.floor(i / cols);
      const baseX = gsap.utils.mapRange(0, cols - 1, -halfW, halfW, col);
      const baseY = gsap.utils.mapRange(0, Math.max(1, rows - 1), -halfH, halfH, row);
      let x, y;
      let attempts = 0;
      do {
        x = baseX + gsap.utils.random(-halfW * 0.1, halfW * 0.1);
        y = baseY + gsap.utils.random(-halfH * 0.12, halfH * 0.12);
        attempts++;
      } while (
        attempts < 20 && (
          (Math.abs(x) < excludeW && Math.abs(y) < excludeH) ||
          placed.some(p => Math.sqrt((x - p.x) ** 2 + (y - p.y) ** 2) < minDistance)
        )
      );
      if (Math.abs(x) < excludeW && Math.abs(y) < excludeH) {
        x = x >= 0 ? excludeW + gsap.utils.random(10, 30) : -(excludeW + gsap.utils.random(10, 30));
        y = y >= 0 ? excludeH + gsap.utils.random(10, 30) : -(excludeH + gsap.utils.random(10, 30));
      }
      const dist = Math.sqrt(x * x + y * y);
      const maxDist = Math.sqrt(halfW * halfW + halfH * halfH);
      const scale = gsap.utils.mapRange(0, maxDist, 1.1, 0.7, dist);
      placed.push({ x, y });
      return { x, y, scale };
    });
  }
  let positions = buildPositions();

  // 3. État initial
  gsap.set(pills, {
    xPercent: -50,
    yPercent: -50,
    x: (i) => positions[i].x,
    y: (i) => positions[i].y,
    scale: (i) => positions[i].scale,
    opacity: 1
  });

  // 4. Timeline scrubée
  const tl = gsap.timeline({
    scrollTrigger: {
      trigger: '.pills_sticky',
      start: 'top bottom',
      end: 'bottom bottom',
      scrub: 0.6,
      invalidateOnRefresh: true,
      onRefresh: () => {
        positions = buildPositions();
        gsap.set(pills, {
          x: (i) => positions[i].x,
          y: (i) => positions[i].y,
          scale: (i) => positions[i].scale,
        });
      }
    }
  });
  tl.to(pills, {
    scale: 0,
    x: 0,
    y: 0,
    opacity: 0,
    stagger: { each: 0.04, from: 'random' },
    duration: 2,
    ease: 'power2.in'
  });
});
</script></div></div><section class="section is-blog"><div class="padding-global padding-5625"><div class="container-large"><div class="section_blogs-featured"><div class="blogs-featured_heading-wrap"><h2>Read our latest articles</h2><a data-wf--btn-secondary--variant="base" href="/blog" class="button-secondary w-inline-block"><p class="btn_txt">See our blog</p><div class="btn_hover"></div></a></div><div class="section_blogs-featured_cards-wrap w-dyn-list"><div role="list" class="section_blogs-featured_cards w-dyn-items"><div role="listitem" class="w-dyn-item"><div class="blogs-featured_card"><div class="blogs-featured_card-media"><img loading="lazy" src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/69b190e7f47e4c44b0ee07d8/6ad9d5868cd7b64b3a340a2d_blog-self-hosted-soc-platform-principles-img-cover%20(2).webp" alt="Three figures&#x27; <script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  var MAX = 2; // nb d'auteurs visibles max

  document.querySelectorAll('.blogs-featured_card').forEach(function (card) {
    var authors = card.querySelectorAll('.blogs-featured_card-author');
    if (authors.length <= MAX) return; // 1 ou 2 auteurs : on ne touche a rien

    // Masquer le 3e auteur et les suivants
    authors.forEach(function (el, i) {
      if (i >= MAX) el.style.display = 'none';
    });

    // 2e auteur -> separateur "&" + nom "more"
    var second = authors[MAX - 1];
    var sep  = second.children[0]; // .text-size-small.text-color-alternate ("By")
    var name = second.children[1]; // .subtitle.s (le nom)
    if (sep)  sep.textContent  = '&';
    if (name) name.textContent = 'more';
  });
});
</script>

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  function clearFilters(field) {
    document.querySelectorAll(`input[fs-list-field="${field}"]`).forEach(input => {
      const checkbox = input.closest('.w-checkbox');
      const custom = checkbox?.querySelector('.w-checkbox-input');
      if (custom?.classList.contains('w--redirected-checked')) {
        custom.click();
      }
    });
  }
  document.querySelector(".ressources_list-clear_btn.type")
    ?.addEventListener("click", () => clearFilters("type"));
  document.querySelector(".ressources_list-clear_btn.product")
    ?.addEventListener("click", () => clearFilters("product"));
});

<script src="https://unpkg.com/lenis@1.2.3/dist/lenis.min.js"></script>
<script>
let lenis = new Lenis({
  lerp: 0.1,
  wheelMultiplier: 0.9,
  gestureOrientation: "vertical",
  normalizeWheel: false,
  smoothTouch: false,
});
function raf(time) {
  lenis.raf(time);
  requestAnimationFrame(raf);
}
requestAnimationFrame(raf);
$("[data-lenis-start]").on("click", function () {
  lenis.start();
});
$("[data-lenis-stop]").on("click", function () {
  lenis.stop();
});
$("[data-lenis-toggle]").on("click", function () {
  $(this).toggleClass("stop-scroll");
  if ($(this).hasClass("stop-scroll")) {
    lenis.stop();
  } else {
    lenis.start();
  }
});

</script>
<script>
/* Pagination Webflow (?xxxx_page=N) — désindexer UNIQUEMENT les pages vides (hors limite).
   Règle : URL paginée + aucune carte d'article rendue -> noindex, follow.
   Ne touche à RIEN sur les pages paginées ayant des articles (canonical inclus). */
(function () {
  if (!/[?&][^=]*_page=\d+/.test(location.search)) return;   // seulement les URLs paginées

  function flagIfEmpty() {
    if (document.querySelector('[fs-list-field="title"]')) return;   // il y a des articles -> ne rien faire
    if (document.querySelector('meta[name="robots"][content*="noindex"]')) return;
    var m = document.createElement('meta');
    m.name = 'robots';
    m.content = 'noindex, follow';
    document.head.appendChild(m);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', flagIfEmpty);
  else flagIfEmpty();
})();
</script></body></html>
